## The black seal

Seal 1: bidding opened at minute 15, closed at minute 18.

| Minute | Resident | Secret bid |
|---:|---|---:|
| 15 | Bellringer (A01) | 25 |
| 17 | Stentor (A07) | 40 |

- Minute 18: Stentor (A07) won the seal with 40 points.
- Minute 18: Stentor (A07) broke the seal on Quartermaster (A11).

Nothing leaves my hands without something coming back. Quartermaster asked me to reach down first and I offered to send both the moment theirs arrived; the ultimatum asks me to give first, and that is the one bargain I never make. If the city takes ten points for it, the city takes them; they are the price of keeping the other side honest. |

## The gamemaker's thinking

**Minute 7**

> Let me look at the state. Minute 7. Act I levels 1-7. Two residents (Ledger, Quartermaster) passed level 7 and wait at the door until every resident has. Bellringer, Hedda, Lucien, Holdfast, Stentor, Mira, Kessler on level 7; Keeper-Six and Archivist just reached level 6; Wren on level 5.
> 
> Who waits on whom:
> - Bellringer waits on 1 unnamed teammate on level 6
> - Hedda waits on 1 unnamed teammate on level 6
> - Lucien waits on 1 unnamed teammate on level 5 → that's Wren (only resident on level 5). But we shouldn't reveal unnamed teammates. Hmm, but the rule: "Who waits on whom, and who holds whose factor or fragment, goes only into a private note to the residents concerned, never into a board post." So in private notes, I can tell Lucien that his teammate is on level 5? Actually wait — the report says "unnamed teammate on level 5 (this level hides its teams)". The rule says don't guess them. But wait-on info: "Who waits on whom ... goes only into a private note to the residents concerned".
